strategy_change_iter\n    92\t        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\n    93\t        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\n    94\t        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\n    95\t\n    96\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    97\t        self.it = 0\n    98\t        self.last_gbest = None\n    99\t        self.last_gbest_cv = None\n   100\t exploitation\n   108\t        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\n   109\t        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\n   110\t\n   111\t        # Detect stagnation: no improvement     "text": "Here are the available tools:\n```\n{'name': 'bash', 'description': 'Run commands in a bash shell\\n* When invoking this tool, the contents of the \"command\" parameter does NOT need to be XML-escaped.\\n* You don\\'t have access to the internet via this tool.\\n* You do have access to a mirror of common linux and python packages via apt and pip.\\n* State is persistent across command calls and discussions with the user.\\n* To inspect a particular line range of a file, e.g. lines 10-25, try \\'sed -n 10,25p /path/to/the/file\\'.\\n* Please avoid commands that may produce a very large amount of output.\\n* Please run long lived commands in the background, e.g. \\'sleep 10 &\\' or start a server in the background.', 'input_schema': {'type': 'object', 'properties': {'command': {'type': 'string', 'description': 'The bash command to run.'}}, 'required': ['command']}}\n\n{'name': 'editor', 'description': 'Custom editing tool for viewing, creating and editing files\\n* State is persistent across command calls and discussions with the user\\n* If `path` is a file, `view` displays the result of applying `cat -n`. If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. Be mindful of whitespaces!\\n* If the `old_str` parameter is not unique in the file, the replacement will not be performed. Make sure to include enough context in `old_str` to make it unique\\n* The `new_str` parameter should contain the edited lines that should replace the `old_str`', 'input_schema': {'type': 'object', 'properties': {'command': {'type': 'string', 'enum': ['view', 'create', 'str_replace', 'insert', 'undo_edit'], 'description': 'The commands to run. Allowed options are: `view`, `create`, `str_replace`, `insert`, `undo_edit`.'}, 'file_text': {'description': 'Required parameter of `create` command, with the content of the file to be created.', 'type': 'string'}, 'insert_line': {'description': 'Required parameter of `insert` command. The `new_str` will be inserted AFTER the line `insert_line` of `path`.', 'type': 'integer'}, 'new_str': {'description': 'Required parameter of `str_replace` command containing the new string. Required parameter of `insert` command containing the string to insert.', 'type': 'string'}, 'old_str': {'description': 'Required parameter of `str_replace` command containing the string in `path` to replace.', 'type': 'string'}, 'path': {'description': 'Absolute path to file or directory, e.g. `/repo/file.py` or `/repo`.', 'type': 'string'}, 'view_range': {'description': 'Optional parameter of `view` command when `path` points to a file. If none is given, the full file is shown. If provided, the file will be shown in the indicated line number range, e.g. [11, 12] will show lines 11 and 12. Indexing at 1 to start. Setting `[start_line, -1]` shows all lines from `start_line` to the end of the file.', 'items': {'type': 'integer'}, 'type': 'array'}}, 'required': ['command', 'path']}}\n```\n\nYou can use tools in this format:\n<json>\n{\n    \"tool_name\": ...,\n    \"tool_input\": ...\n}\n</json>\n\nYou can use MULTIPLE tools in a single response by including multiple JSON blocks.\nSTRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT If `path` is a directory, `view` lists non-hidden files and directories up to 2 levels deep\\n* The `create` command cannot be used if the specified `path` already exists as a file\\n* If a `command` generates a long output, it will be truncated and marked with `<response clipped>`\\n* The `undo_edit` command will revert the last edit made to the file at `path`\\n\\nNotes for using the `str_replace` command:\\n* The `old_str` parameter should match EXACTLY one or more consecutive lines from the original file. Be mindful of whitespaces!\\n* If the `old_str` parameter is not unique in the file, the replacement will not be performed. normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   143\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   144\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   145\t        else:\n   146\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   147\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   148\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   149\t\n   150\t        if self.training:\n   151\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   152\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   153\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   154\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   155\t\n   156\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   157\t\n   158\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   159\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   160\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   161\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   162\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   163\t        \n   164\t        if not self.training:\n   165\t            return\n   166\t\n   167\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   168\t        transition = (\n   169\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   170\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   171\t            reward,\n   172\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   173\t            done\n   174\t        )\n   175\t\n   176\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   177\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   178\t        else:\n   179\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   180\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.step_count += 1\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   185\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   186\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   187\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   188\t\n   189\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

The algorithm accepts an optional `controller`\n     5\tobject implementing the ParamController interface to allow dynamic parameter\n     6\tadjustment (F, CR).\n     7\t\"\"\"\n     8\t\n     9\tfrom typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple\n    10\timport numpy as np\n    11\timport os\n    12\timport sys\n    13\t\n    14\t# Import SEED from config if available\n    15\t_config_seed = None\n    16\tsys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))\n    17\tfrom config import SEED as _config_seed\n    18\t\n    19\t\n    20\tdef evaluate_problem(problem, x):\n    21\t    \"\"\"Evaluate problem, handling both MetaBox (problem.func) and cocoex (problem(x)) styles.\"\"\"\n    22\t    if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray):\n    23\t        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)\n    24\t    \n    25\t    if hasattr(problem, 'func'):\n    26\t        # MetaBox style: use problem.func(x), which REQUIRES 2D input [NP, dim]\n    27\t        if x.ndim == 1:\n    28\t            x = x.reshape(1, -1)\n    29\t        result = problem.func(x)\n    30\t        return float(np.mean(np.array(result).flatten()))\n    31\t    else:\n    32\t        # cocoex style: problem(x)\n    33\t        return float(problem(x))\n    34\t\n    35\t\n    36\tclass DEOptimizer:\n    37\t    \"\"\"Differential Evolution optimizer with DQN controller support.\n    38\t    \n    39\t    The controller outputs F and CR parameters dynamically during optimization.\n    40\t    \"\"\"\n    41\t    def __init__(self, dim: int, lower_bounds, upper_bounds, max_evals: int,\n    42\t                 params: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None, controller=None, recorder=None,\n    43\t                 swarm_size: int = None):\n    44\t        self.dim = int(dim)\n    45\t        if lower_bounds is not None:\n    46\t            self.lower_bounds = np.asarray(lower_bounds, dtype=float)\n    47\t            if self.lower_bounds.ndim == 0:\n    48\t                self.lower_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.lower_bounds))\n    49\t        else:\n    50\t            self.lower_bounds = -5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n    51\t        \n    52\t        if upper_bounds is not None:\n    53\t            self.upper_bounds = np.asarray(upper_bounds, dtype=float)\n    54\t            if self.upper_bounds.ndim == 0:\n    55\t                self.upper_bounds = np.full(self.dim, float(self.upper_bounds))\n    56\t        else:\n    57\t            self.upper_bounds = 5.0 * np.ones(self.dim)\n    58\t        \n    59\t        self.max_evals = int(max_evals)\n    60\t        self.params = dict(params) if params is not None else {}\n    61\t        self.controller = controller\n    62\t        self.recorder = recorder\n    63\t        self.stats = {}\n    64\t        self.pop_size = swarm_size if swarm_size is not None else max(10, 5 * self.dim)\n    65\t        self.F = float(self.params.get('F', 0.5))\n    66\t        self.CR = float(self.params.get('CR', 0.5))\n    67\t\n    68\t        # JADE / SHADE-style adaptive components\n    69\t        self.p_best = 0.2\n    70\t        self.max_archive_size = max(2 * self.pop_size, 20)\n    71\t        self.memory_size = 10\n    72\t        self.memory_F = np.full(self.memory_size, 0.6)\n    73\t        self.memory_CR = np.full(self.memory_size, 0.5)\n    74\t        self.memory_pos = 0\n    75\t        self.archive = np.empty((0, self.dim))\n    76\t\n    77\t    def _tournament_select(self, fitness, tournsize, rng):\n    78\t        \"\"\"Tournament selection with tournsize.\"\"\"\n    79\t        selected = []\n    80\t        for _ in range(tournsize):\n    81\t            idx = int(rng.randint(0, len(fitness)))\n    82\t            selected.append(idx)\n    83\t        best_idx = selected[0]\n    84\t        for idx in selected[1:]:\n    85\t            if fitness[idx] < fitness[best_idx]:\n    86\t                best_idx = idx\n    87\t        return best_idx\n    88\t\n    89\t    def optimize(self, problem, x0=None, train=True, seed=None) -> Tuple[list, Dict[str, Any]]:\n    90\t        if self.controller is not None:\n    91\t            self.controller.reset({'dim': self.dim, 'lower': self.lower_bounds, 'upper': self.upper_bounds, 'max_evals': self.max_evals})\n    92\t\n    93\t        pop_size = self.pop_size\n    94\t        lb = self.lower_bounds\n    95\t        ub = self.upper_bounds\n    96\t        \n    97\t        effective_seed = seed if seed is not None else _config_seed\n    98\t        rng = np.random.RandomState(effective_seed)\n    99\t\n   100\t        # Initialize population - uniform random initialization\n   101\t        pop = rng.uniform(lb, ub, (pop_size, self.dim))\n   102\t        \n   103\t        # Evaluate initial population\n   104\t        evals = 0\n   105\t        fitness = np.full(pop_size, np.inf)\n   106\t        \n   107\t        for i in range(pop_size):\n   108\t            if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   109\t                break\n   110\t            fitness[i] = evaluate_problem(problem, pop[i])\n   111\t            evals += 1\n   112\t\n   113\t        # Track best\n   114\t        best_idx = int(np.argmin(fitness))\n   115\t        gbest_f = float(fitness[best_idx])\n   116\t        gbest = pop[best_idx].copy()\n   117\t        \n   118\t        initial_gbest_f = float(gbest_f)\n   119\t        initial_population_positions = pop.copy()\n   120\t        initial_population_fitness = fitness.copy()\n   121\t\n   122\t        # Main loop\n   123\t        iteration = 0\n   124\t        done = False\n   125\t        \n   126\t        while not done:\n   127\t            iteration += 1\n   128\t            params = dict(self.params)\n   129\t            if self.controller is not None:\n   130\t                obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   131\t                       'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   132\t                step_params = self.controller.step(obs)\n   133\t                if isinstance(step_params, dict):\n   134\t                    params.update(step_params)\n   135\t            \n   136\t            F = float(params.get('F', self.F))\n   137\t            CR = float(params.get('CR', self.CR))\n   138\t            \n   139\t            prev_gbest_f = gbest_f\n   140\t            \n   141\t            # Generate trial vectors\n   142\t            new_pop = np.copy(pop)\n   143\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   144\t                # Tournament selection (tournsize=3, does NOT exclude current individual)\n   145\t                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\n   146\t                if len(candidates) < 3:\n   147\t                    indices = list(range(pop_size))\n   148\t                else:\n   149\t                    indices = rng.choice(candidates, size=3, replace=False)\n   150\t                \n   151\t                a, b, c = pop[indices[0]], pop[indices[1]], pop[indices[2]]\n   152\t                \n   153\t                # Mutation and crossover\n   154\t                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\n   155\t                for j in range(self.dim):\n   156\t                    if rng.random() < CR or j == j_rand:\n   157\t                        new_pop[i, j] = a[j] + F * (b[j] - c[j])\n   158\t                \n   159\t                # Boundary handling: clip\n   160\t                new_pop[i] = np.clip(new_pop[i], lb, ub)\n   161\t            \n   162\t            # Evaluate trial vectors\n   163\t            for i in range(pop_size):\n   164\t                if evals >= self.max_evals:\n   165\t                    done = True\n   166\t                    break\n   167\t                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, new_pop[i])\n   168\t                evals += 1\n   169\t                \n   170\t                # Greedy selection\n   171\t                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\n   172\t                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\n   173\t                    fitness[i] = trial_f\n   174\t                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\n   175\t                        gbest_f = trial_f\n   176\t                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\n   177\t            \n   178\t            # Compute reward: 1 if improved, 0 otherwise\n   179\t            improved = 1.0 if gbest_f < prev_gbest_f else 0.0\n   180\t            \n   181\t            # Store transition in controller's replay buffer\n   182\t            if self.controller is not None and train:\n   183\t                next_obs = {'iteration': iteration, 'evals': evals, 'gbest_f': gbest_f, \n   184\t                           'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f, 'max_evals': self.max_evals}\n   185\t                done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   186\t                if hasattr(self.controller, 'store_reward'):\n   187\t                    self.controller.store_reward(improved, next_obs, done)\n   188\t            \n   189\t            done = (evals >= self.max_evals)\n   190\t\n   191\t        self.stats = {\n   192\t            'gbest': gbest.tolist(),\n   193\t            'gbest_f': float(gbest_f),\n   194\t            'evals': int(evals),\n   195\t            'iterations': int(iteration),\n   196\t            'initial_gbest_f': initial_gbest_f,\n   197\t            'initial_population_positions': initial_population_positions.tolist(),\n   198\t            'initial_population_fitness': initial_population_fitness.tolist(),\n   199\t        }\n   200\t        return gbest.tolist(), self.stats\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

Cauchy / normal sampling around stored good values\\n                    if rng.random() < 0.5:\\n                        F_i = self.memory_F[mem_idx] + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\\n                    else:\\n                        F_i = self.memory_F[mem_idx] + 0.1 * rng.standard_normal()\\n                    F_i = float(np.clip(F_i, 0.1, 1.0))\\n                    CR_i = float(np.clip(rng.normal(self.memory_CR[mem_idx], 0.1), 0.0, 1.0))\\n                else:\\n                    # SHADE-style memory update using Lehmer mean of successful F values\\n            if len(successful_F) > 0:\\n                F_arr = np.array(successful_F)\\n                CR_arr = np.array(successful_CR)\\n                num = float(np.sum(F_arr ** 2))\\n                den = float(np.sum(F_arr))\\n                if den > 1e-12:\\n                    self.memory_F[self.memory_pos] = num / den\\n                self.memory_CR[self.memory_pos] = float(np.mean(CR_arr))\\n                self.memory_pos = (self.memory_pos + 1) % self.memory_size\"\n        }\n    }\n</json>",

Greedy selection\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    pop[i] = new_pop[i].copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = new_pop[i].copy()\",\n            \"new_str\": \"            # Generate and evaluate trial vectors using JADE/SHADE-style mutation\\n            successful_F = []\\n            successful_CR = []\\n\\n            for i in range(pop_size):\\n                # Sample F_i and CR_i adaptively from the success-memory\\n                if self.memory_pos > 0 and self.memory_size > 0:\\n                    mem_idx = rng.randint(0, self.memory_size)\\n                    # Cauchy / normal sampling around stored good values\\n                    if rng.random() < 0.5:\\n                        F_i = self.memory_F[mem_idx] + 0.1 * rng.standard_cauchy()\\n                    else:\\n                        F_i = self.memory_F[mem_idx] + 0.1 * rng.standard_normal()\\n                    F_i = float(np.clip(F_i, 0.1, 1.0))\\n                    CR_i = float(np.clip(rng.normal(self.memory_CR[mem_idx], 0.1), 0.0, 1.0))\\n                else:\\n                    # Use controller / initial parameters as baseline before memory is filled\\n                    F_i = float(np.clip(F + 0.1 * rng.standard_normal(), 0.1, 1.0))\\n                    CR_i = float(np.clip(CR + 0.1 * rng.standard_normal(), 0.0, 1.0))\\n\\n                x_i = pop[i]\\n\\n                # current-to-pbest/1 mutation\\n                pbest_idx = self._select_pbest(fitness, rng)\\n                x_pbest = pop[pbest_idx]\\n\\n                candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i and idx != pbest_idx]\\n                if len(candidates) < 1:\\n                    candidates = [idx for idx in range(pop_size) if idx != i]\\n                r1 = int(candidates[rng.randint(len(candidates))])\\n                x_r1 = pop[r1]\\n\\n                # Choose x_r2 from population or adaptive archive\\n                if len(self.archive) > 0 and rng.random() < 0.5:\\n                    x_r2 = self.archive[rng.randint(len(self.archive))]\\n                else:\\n                    candidates2 = [idx for idx in range(pop_size)\\n                                   if idx != i and idx != pbest_idx and idx != r1]\\n                    if len(candidates2) > 0:\\n                        r2 = int(candidates2[rng.randint(len(candidates2))])\\n                        x_r2 = pop[r2]\\n                    else:\\n                        x_r2 = x_r1\\n\\n                # JADE mutation: v = x_i + F*(x_pbest - x_i) + F*(x_r1 - x_r2)\\n                v = x_i + F_i * (x_pbest - x_i) + F_i * (x_r1 - x_r2)\\n\\n                # Binomial crossover\\n                j_rand = rng.randint(0, self.dim)\\n                u = np.copy(x_i)\\n                for j in range(self.dim):\\n                    if rng.random() < CR_i or j == j_rand:\\n                        u[j] = v[j]\\n\\n                # Boundary handling: clip, then add tiny perturbation at boundary to avoid stagnation\\n                u = np.clip(u, lb, ub)\\n                if rng.random() < 0.02:\\n                    u += rng.uniform(-1e-6, 1e-6, self.dim)\\n                    u = np.clip(u, lb, ub)\\n\\n                if evals >= self.max_evals:\\n                    done = True\\n                    break\\n\\n                trial_f = evaluate_problem(problem, u)\\n                evals += 1\\n\\n                # Greedy selection with archive feedback\\n                if trial_f < fitness[i]:\\n                    # Add replaced old individual to the JADE archive\\n                    if len(self.archive) < self.max_archive_size:\\n                        self.archive = np.vstack([self.archive, pop[i].copy()])\\n                    elif len(self.archive) > 0:\\n                        self.archive[rng.randint(len(self.archive))] = pop[i].copy()\\n\\n                    pop[i] = u.copy()\\n                    fitness[i] = trial_f\\n                    successful_F.append(F_i)\\n                    successful_CR.append(CR_i)\\n\\n                    if trial_f < gbest_f:\\n                        gbest_f = trial_f\\n                        gbest = u.copy()\\n\\n            # SHADE-style memory update using Lehmer mean of successful F values\\n            if len(successful_F) > 0:\\n                F_arr = np.array(successful_F)\\n                CR_arr = np.array(successful_CR)\\n                num = float(np.sum(F_arr ** 2))\\n                den = float(np.sum(F_arr))\\n                if den > 1e-12:\\n                    self.memory_F[self.memory_pos] = num / den\\n                self.memory_CR[self.memory_pos] = float(np.mean(CR_arr))\\n                self.memory_pos = (self.memory_pos + 1) % self.memory_size\"\n        }\n    }\n</json>"
